- **mcd-optimal-order**: 麦当劳「怎么买最划算」的跨渠道比价与最优下单技能。当用户说「帮我点麦麦」「今天吃麦当劳怎么最省」「外送还是自提划算」 「我有券能不能用」「预算 30 块含汉堡怎么配」「热量别超过 800 大卡」「两家店哪个便宜」「团餐怎么点」时使用。 技能会先探测可用渠道（到店自取 / 麦乐送 / 团餐 / 得来速），按渠道分别拉菜单并做跨渠道商品对齐（商品编码跨渠道不通用）， 生成组合候选，再用 calculate-price 官方核价，最后对券做「门店 × 渠道 × 商品 × 时段」四维核销判定，输出 3 套可比方案 + 逐项拆解 + 解释链 + dry-run 下单草案。默认不下单、不抽奖、不兑换，花钱动作必须人工确认。 已验证 35 个麦当劳 MCP 工具中 18 个的真实入参、返回字段与业务错误码语义（60006/60010/60012/60047/60050）。 ([Shiroboki/mcd-optimum](https://github.com/Shiroboki/mcd-optimum/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **mcd-order**: Find the cheapest way for a group to order McDonald's China (麦当劳): treat combos as cheap component containers, redistribute their parts across people, and use coupons (优惠券) and points (积分) to cover everyone's exact items for the least cash. - **mcm-humanizer**: 美赛(MCM/ICM)数学建模论文去AI味工具。专门针对美赛论文的写作风格优化， 降低AI检测率，同时保持学术严谨性。支持Summary、Model、Results等各部分优化。 ([ychenfen/claude-agent-skills-collection](https://github.com/ychenfen/claude-agent-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/academic-modeling/mcm-humanizer) · 2 copies)
- **mcm-icm-competition-paper**: Use when planning, executing, reviewing, or repairing an MCM/ICM mathematical modeling paper or simulation workflow. This skill governs Codex behavior for contest-style papers: problem interpretation, external data acquisition, model design, figure/table planning, LaTeX/PDF production, visual QA, judge-style review, and release readiness. It is especially relevant when the user asks to run an MCM/ICM project end-to-end, improve a poor paper, create a workflow diagram, or decide whether an automation Kit pass is enough. ([echo5177/MCM-ICM-Automation-Kit](https://github.com/echo5177/MCM-ICM-Automation-Kit/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **mcm-latex-format**: Use when 需要为美赛 MCM/ICM 论文搭建 LaTeX 骨架、排版初稿或做格式合规检查。Triggers include 论文排版、LaTeX 格式、格式合规、美赛 LaTeX 模板、LaTeX skeleton、format the paper、COMAP LaTeX template、summary sheet 排版、页眉设置、\documentclass 选型。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-latex-format))
- **mcm-master**: Full-stack autonomous math modeling agent for MCM/ICM (美国大学生数学建模竞赛). Handles team control number, problem choice (A–F), English academic writing, mcmthesis LaTeX template, and optional practical deliverable (memo/letter/report). Use when the user provides an MCM/ICM problem and wants end-to-end automated modeling, coding, and paper generation in English. ([RealSeaberry/AutoMCM-Pro](https://github.com/RealSeaberry/AutoMCM-Pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-master) · ★266 · 3 copies)
- **mcm-math-modeling**: Runs an end-to-end mathematical modeling competition workflow (MCM/ICM, CUMCM, and regional Chinese contests) from problem statement to a submission-ready paper, enforced by executable quality checkpoints. Use when the user mentions math modeling, 数模, 建模, MCM, ICM, COMAP, CUMCM, 国赛, 美赛, or asks to analyze a competition problem, select a model, solve it, generate figures, or write and check a contest paper. ([hongdotien10-hash/mathmodel-toolkit](https://github.com/hongdotien10-hash/mathmodel-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/mcm-math-modeling))
- **mcm-memo**: Use when **题面明确要求 letter/memo（备忘录）**时要起草或复核一份面向指定受众的一页备忘录（letter / memo）。★ **题面没要求就绝不产出**（`[社区]` 操作纪律）—— 它是**题目特定要求**，不是通用交付物。Triggers include 写备忘录、写memo、备忘录、letter、memo、给某个机构的一封信、letter to、memo to、one-page memo、非技术性信件。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-memo))
- **mcm-model-select**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 建模阶段要从题面特征选出候选模型，或需要在几个模型之间说清"为什么用它 / 什么时候不该用它"。贴题面后，走两跳（先选类、再选方法），产出候选模型 + 判据 + 一个带推翻条件的推荐 + 被选中模型的六格详情与可跑骨架路径。Triggers include 选模型、建模方法、用什么模型、model selection、choose a model、候选模型、模型库、该建什么模型、建哪个模型、modeling approach、which model。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-model-select))
- **mcm-modeling**: 数学建模竞赛全流程主管指令（dsh 侧）。用于启动/执行竞赛任务：拆解→建模→制图→写作→审查→预审合规。触发词：建模、赛题、国赛、CUMCM、拆解、任务卡。适合决策类/数据处理类赛题；搭配 liangshen preset 使用效果最佳。 ([RudesTheCat/MCM_Workshop](https://github.com/RudesTheCat/MCM_Workshop/tree/HEAD/.dsh/skills/mcm-modeling) · ★1)
- **mcm-o-guide**: MCM/ICM 优秀论文写作全流程指南——基于 502 篇历年获奖论文（2004–2024）语料库的结构化分析与评委点评提炼。覆盖：摘要逐句模板、6 类题型模型选择矩阵、25 页页预算分配、图表黄金法则、灵敏度/稳健性分析范式、全文一致性检查清单。当用户准备美赛、写论文、打磨论文、查结构、选模型、做摘要、看评委标准、读优秀论文时使用。也适用于任何需要高质量数学建模论文写作的场景。 ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/all-skills/skills/mcm-o-guide) · ★3.8k · 2 copies)
- **mcm-paper-editor**: 美赛/数模论文逐节修改润色，按题型（ABC/DEF）提供模板，含语言风格与可视化规范。 ([J0Jng/mcm-paper-editor](https://github.com/J0Jng/mcm-paper-editor/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **mcm-playbook**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 赛程中要回答"现在该干什么"——拿到开赛时刻与当前进展，要判断处在哪个阶段、这一刻该做哪几件事、该调哪个已建 skill、这个阶段最容易栽在哪，或赛程推进中拿不准"按表走还是按进展走"时。Triggers include 赛程、时间线、现在该干什么、接下来做什么、下一步做什么、阶段、进度、还剩多久、开赛流程、赛程编排、playbook、timeline、schedule、what should I do now。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-playbook))
- **mcm-plot-matlab**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 论文要用 MATLAB 出图、把图型决策落成 MATLAB 代码，或已画出的图被出货检查器打回（图宽比不合、底色是深色、字体族不对）需要按规范重画时。Triggers include MATLAB 画图、matlab 出图、MATLAB 代码出图、按规范画图、figure 出图、print 导出、底色太深、图宽不对、字体不对、plot the figure in MATLAB。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-plot-matlab))

- **mcm-plot-origin**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 论文要用 Origin 出图、把图型决策落成 Origin 脚本，或已画出的图被出货检查器打回（图宽比不合、底色不是白、显式色序不对）需要按规范重画时。Triggers include Origin 画图、origin 出图、Origin 代码出图、OriginPro 出图、按规范画图、底色不对、图宽不对、颜色不对、plot the figure in Origin。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-plot-origin))

- **mcm-schematic**: Use when 要把一段"过程 / 结构 / 机理"的文字描述落成美赛 MCM/ICM 论文里可直接粘的 TikZ 示意图源码（技术路线图 / 机理示意图 / 模型结构图），或已画好的示意图被出货检查器打回（图宽不合、底色不是白、显著色不在允许色序、图注不合规、字体族不对）需要按规范重画时。Triggers include 示意图、技术路线图、机理图、模型结构图、流程图、框图、TikZ、tikzpicture、画结构图、画流程图、schematic、flow chart、block diagram、LaTeX 画图、示意图规范。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-schematic))

- **mcm-section-writer**: Use when 你正在写美赛 MCM/ICM 论文的**某一节正文**（不是整篇）——手上已有题面与该节要点清单，要"这一节该回答什么、按什么次序组织、最容易在哪几条纪律上翻车、哪些内容不属于这一节"时。Triggers include 写正文、写某一节、这一节怎么写、这一节该写什么、按节写作、正文起草、section、write a section、draft this section、论文正文。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-section-writer))
- **mcm-selfreview**: Use when 需要在提交前对美赛 MCM/ICM 论文稿做最后自查，或判断"能不能提交"。Triggers include 论文自查、投稿前检查、提交前检查、能不能提交、final check、pre-submission check、paper review before submission、compliance check、格式合规检查、美赛合规自查。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-selfreview))
- **mcm-table**: 假 skill（Task 7 的变异 fixture）：只用来证明 `check-spec-pointers.py` 的家族正则 `FAMILY_RE` 里 `mcm-table` 这条备选**真的在射程内**（缺指针 ⇒ `K2` 判红）。不是交付物。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/tests/skills/figure-choose/fixtures/fake-skills/mcm-table))
- **mcm-table**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 论文要排版表格、把数据落成可直接粘进论文的 LaTeX 表格代码，或已排好的表被出货检查器打回（缺三线结构、表宽溢出、表题位置不对、来源没回显）需要按规范重排时。Triggers include 表格、三线表、LaTeX 表格、排版表格、booktabs、siunitx、表格代码、LaTeX table、表格超宽、表格规范、format the table、tabular。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-table))
- **mcm-topic-select**: Use when 美赛 MCM/ICM 开赛后要从六道题里挑一道、并说清"为什么是它"——贴当天六道题的题面，要一份「每题按语料口径的分类 + 2016–2026 同型历史 + 四轴证据档 + 一个带推翻条件的推荐」时。Triggers include 选题、选题决策、选哪道题、挑题、定题、哪题好做、第一小时干什么、六题怎么选、topic select、which problem、pick a problem、choose the problem。 ([241880199/MathModel](https://github.com/241880199/MathModel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mcm-topic-select))
- **mcm-writing**: MCM/ICM paper writing guide built on the bundled template_en. Activate only when the coordinator has identified an English MCM/ICM report. Provides file-by-file instructions tied to the LaTeX template structure. Enforces English output and forbids fallback from CUMCM/template_zh failures. ([reaslab/MathModelingAgent-CodexBundle](https://github.com/reaslab/MathModelingAgent-CodexBundle/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mcm_writing) · ★10)
- **mcmaster-parts**: Use for any bought hardware in a CAD design (bearings, screws, nuts, washers, heat-set inserts, dowel and shoulder pins, standoffs and spacers, rod ends, threaded rod, grommets). Always choose a real McMaster-Carr part with a part number and place its manufacturer STEP model, normalised into a known frame, instead of drawing an envelope; record the part, its full specification table, price and mass in the project's hardware catalogue. ([Valpatel/cad-agent](https://github.com/Valpatel/cad-agent/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mcmaster-parts) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **mcmaster-parts**: Find McMaster-Carr hardware (screws, nuts, bearings, dowel pins, shafts, shaft collars, standoffs, inserts, springs) with the McMaster MCP, download the native SolidWorks CAD (.SLDPRT) or STEP, file it in the project, and open it or insert it into the active SolidWorks assembly. Use when the user asks for McMaster parts, off-the-shelf hardware CAD, a bill of materials of purchased parts, or to "add bearings/screws" to a SolidWorks model, including the bearings and pins called out by the gearbox plugins. ([adityamahes/solidworks-plugins](https://github.com/adityamahes/solidworks-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/sw-mcmaster-parts/skills/mcmaster-parts))
- **mcmc-and-prediction**: Run Pyro HMC/NUTS/MCMC diagnostics and prior/posterior predictive sampling workflows. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/pyro/sub-skills/mcmc-and-prediction) · ★357)
- **mcmc-and-sampling**: Use ZhuSuan for HMC, SGLD, PSGLD, SGHMC, SGNHT, annealed importance sampling, and posterior-sampling diagnostics. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/zhusuan/sub-skills/mcmc-and-sampling) · ★357)
- **mcmc-autocorrelation-time-analysis**: Use when comparing sampler efficiency (e.g. emcee/CosmoHammer vs Metropolis-Hastings CosmoMC) by estimating autocorrelation times per parameter, or when determining the sampling effort needed to reach a target statistical error epsilon &lt;= 0.1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-autocorrelation-time-analysis))
- **mcmc-based-bayesian-mixture-modeling**: Use when clustering raw UMI count matrices from multiple individuals (each a genes x cells matrix in a list) where individual-level heterogeneity/batch effects exist, or when a trained model must predict cluster labels for held-out cells via a training-proportion split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-based-bayesian-mixture-modeling))
- **mcmc-based-hyperparameter-inference**: Use when Canopy2 requires the 1 x M alpha and beta hyperparameter vectors and single-cell expression counts are available for the mutation-host genes (e.g., the featurecounts.qc slot of GBM10_preproc). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-based-hyperparameter-inference))
- **mcmc-based-peak-parameter-estimation**: Use when 1D NMR spectra (especially highly overlapped metabolomics spectra) must be deconvoluted into peak tables with accurate peak parameters rather than fitted in the frequency domain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mcmc-based-peak-parameter-estimation))
- **mcmc-based-spatial-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing computational efficiency of spatial transcriptomics clustering methods (e.g. iSC.MEB vs MCMC-based BASS/BayesSpace) across multiple tissue sections and a sequence of K cluster numbers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-based-spatial-clustering-benchmarking))
- **mcmc-chain-convergence-and-marginal-posterior-analysis**: Use when a C++/CBL analysis needs posterior sampling of cosmological model parameters via MCMC, with a chi-squared likelihood plus Gaussian priors, and chain/posterior outputs must be validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-chain-convergence-and-marginal-posterior-analysis))
- **mcmc-context-data-exchange-design**: Use when building an extensible MCMC framework that must combine independent theory-prediction (core) and likelihood modules via a shared context, or when refactoring a monolithic likelihood computation into plug-in modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-context-data-exchange-design))
- **mcmc-convergence-and-posterior-constraining**: Use when a radial-velocity time series contains an unknown number of planetary signals and you need the sampler itself to decide how many Keplerian components to keep, with posteriors from each run seeding the next and a tolerance-based stopping rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-convergence-and-posterior-constraining))
- **mcmc-convergence-diagnostic-analysis**: Use when comparing two sampler backends (e.g. emcee ensemble MCMC vs stan HMC/NUTS) on the same visibility likelihood, or when deciding whether a posterior run has converged before trusting parameter estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-convergence-diagnostic-analysis))
- **mcmc-convergence-diagnostic-tabulation**: Use when you have posterior chains from ensemble (Goodman-Weare/emcee) or Metropolis-Hastings MCMC samplers and need per-parameter autocorrelation times to compare sampler convergence and efficiency side by side. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-convergence-diagnostic-tabulation))
- **mcmc-convergence-diagnostics**: Use when fitting Stan hierarchical/binomial multilevel models (e.g., PrediXcan-DEG enrichment or proportion-positive direction-of-effect models) and you must verify chain convergence before extracting marginal posterior means or 95% posterior-mass flags. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mcmc-convergence-diagnostics--953f264322b4498e))
- **mcmc-convergence-diagnostics**: Use when running a Gibbs sampler over a candidate-assignment network (e.g., ZODIAC reranking SIRIUS formula candidates) and you must set chain count, burn-in, sampling epochs, and verify the chains have stabilized before trusting posterior marginals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mcmc-convergence-diagnostics--ab0ee83628e1c368))
- **mcmc-convergence-diagnostics**: Use when running get_trees() MCMC sampling in Canopy2 across a Klist of subclone counts and you must verify chain mixing and convergence before get__tree() selects the minimum-BIC configuration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-convergence-diagnostics))
- **mcmc-convergence-verification-against-reference**: Use when an MCMC posterior sample of cosmological parameters (e.g. from CosmoHammer/emcee on WMAP 7 data) must be cross-checked against a reference sampler like CosmoMC to confirm convergence and statistical agreement within ~3.4%-of-sigma tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-convergence-verification-against-reference))
- **mcmc-diagnostics**: MCMC convergence diagnostics and analysis ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/domains/science/mathematics/skills/mcmc-diagnostics) · ★1.9k)
- **mcmc-diagnostics**: Run and troubleshoot NumPyro MCMC workflows with diagnostics, predictive sampling, reparameterization, and JAX chain configuration. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/numpyro/sub-skills/mcmc-diagnostics) · ★357)
- **mcmc-diagnostics**: Master MCMC convergence diagnostics with R-hat, ESS, trace plots, and divergence checking. Use when validating Bayesian inference results from Turing.jl. Also use when chains look poorly mixed, R-hat is too high, effective sample size is low, or divergent transitions appear. Use proactively when interpreting posterior samples, debugging sampler performance, or deciding whether to reparameterize a model, even if the user only mentions "my MCMC isn't converging." ([imewei/MyClaude](https://github.com/imewei/MyClaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/science-suite/skills/mcmc-diagnostics) · ★1)
- **mcmc-eclipse-fitting-benchmark-eval**: This benchmark evaluates the accuracy and reliability of Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) light curve fitting routines for detecting and measuring exoplanet secondary eclipses. It probes how well analysis pipelines recover known eclipse depths and phase centers under varying noise conditions, including synthetic white/red noise and real observational systematics. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MCMC Eclipse Benchmark Suite, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports eclipse depth. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mcmc-eclipse-fitting-benchmark-eval) · ★3)
- **mcmc-ensemble-mean-tracking**: Use when you need to quantify how many MCMC iterations are required before chains lose their initialisation bias, especially when comparing ensemble samplers with large walker counts L against Metropolis–Hastings chains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-ensemble-mean-tracking))
- **mcmc-ensemble-sampling-cosmological-parameters**: Use when you need to estimate cosmological parameters (e.g. the seven Lambda-CDM parameters) from CMB data via MCMC, or benchmark a new sampler's posteriors against a CosmoMC reference chain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-ensemble-sampling-cosmological-parameters))
- **mcmc-gibbs-sampling**: Use when fitting Kepler or ground-based transit light curves where classic chi-squared underestimates parameter uncertainties because the photometric noise contains a correlated (red) component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-gibbs-sampling))
- **mcmc-gibbs-sampling-inference**: Use when you need per-gene or per-cluster kinetic parameter distributions (kon, koff, s) estimated from real scRNA-seq count data to parameterize a two-state transcription bursting model, e.g. for SymSim simulation inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-gibbs-sampling-inference))
- **mcmc-hierarchical-dcm-clustering**: Markov chain Monte Carlo methods for hierarchical clustering of dynamic causal models, enabling subgroup detection in heterogeneous populations through effective brain connectivity analysis. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/reasoning-bayesian/mcmc-hierarchical-dcm-clustering) · ★4)
- **mcmc-hyperparameter-and-burnin-tuning**: Use when fitting a Bayesian DTM regression on compositional microbiome data (e.g. genus-level OTU counts) and you must choose inclusion-prior hyperparameters, MCMC run length/burn-in, and association-selection cutoffs before comparing variable-selection counts across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mcmc-hyperparameter-and-burnin-tuning))
- **mcmc-hyperparameter-configuration**: Use when running Bfimpute on scRNA-seq count matrices and you must set or tune the MCMC factorization settings (D, totalepoch, burnin, threshold, sn_init/sn_max, priors) to reproduce published benchmarks or trade accuracy vs. compute time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-hyperparameter-configuration))
- **mcmc-lineage-refinement**: Use when you need to reproduce or unit-test VIA's pseudotime computation and forward-biased MCMC lineage refinement on a PARC cluster graph (e.g. mESC CyTOF cell-cycle data) with explicit x_lazy/alpha_teleport settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mcmc-lineage-refinement))
- **mcmc-population-chain-initialization**: Use when sampling from a high-dimensional Bayesian posterior in real parameter space where choosing jump scale/orientation for standard MCMC is hard, and WinBUGS-style one-dimensional updates are too limited. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-population-chain-initialization))
- **mcmc-posterior-inference**: Use when posterior samples of covariate-taxon or mediation-effect inclusion are needed from compositional count data via MicroBVS MCMC (DMbvs_R, MCMC_Med) before applying selected_DM or Selection_Med1/Med2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-inference))
- **mcmc-posterior-marginalization**: Use when you need to marginalize over nuisance parameters (e.g., 63 log-normal nuclear-rate uncertainties) to obtain light-element abundance posteriors from a BBN forward model like PRyMordial, comparing rate prescriptions such as PRIMAT vs NACRE II. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-marginalization))
- **mcmc-posterior-parameter-estimation**: Use when reanalyzing Kepler long-cadence (29.4 min) transit photometry to derive Period, Inclination, a/Rs, and Rp/Rs posteriors via MCMC, or when comparing independent transit fits with a fractional-difference significance test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-parameter-estimation--625da6f013605ea9))
- **mcmc-posterior-parameter-estimation**: Use when you have multi-instrument radial-velocity data (e.g. 51peg.vels from LICK) and need posterior parameter estimates plus Bayesian model selection (ln Z, BIC) over increasing Keplerian counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-parameter-estimation--343de017209c45bf))
- **mcmc-posterior-percentile-summarization**: Use when posterior summaries of a Bayesian hierarchical/nonlinear mixed-effects model must be reported as percentiles and checked against a reference sampler's output for agreement within Monte Carlo error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-percentile-summarization))
- **mcmc-posterior-sampling**: Use when fitting parametric radial profiles (e.g. Gaussian, power) to ALMA visibility sets exported from an ms file and you need posterior parameter estimates via MCMC rather than a single optimisation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-sampling--357022537bc78ddc))
- **mcmc-posterior-sampling**: Use when fitting a 1D (optionally beam-smeared) galaxy rotation curve to recover physical mass-model parameters, or when validating MCMC parameter recovery on synthetic rotation curves at known S/N levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/mcmc-posterior-sampling--6646fbe68ab421ea))
- **mcmc-prior-estimation-single-cell**: Use when analyzing allele-specific expression (ASE) from scRNA-Seq allele counts (e.g., CAST×B6 F1 embryo data) and you need population-informed priors to overcome low per-cell coverage before assigning P/B/M expression states per gene per cell. - **mcmc-sampler-performance-comparison**: Use when benchmarking an ensemble MCMC sampler against a self-tuning Metropolis–Hastings sampler: you have autocorrelation times tau, burn-in length b, walker count L and sequential steps n, and need to find at what sampling effort epsilon &lt;= 0.1.
